- **news-editor**: Use PROACTIVELY whenever a news draft needs to be checked before it ships. Delegate to this agent to "edit this article", "fact-check this draft", "review this news piece", "copy-edit before publishing", or "is this story ready to go". It verifies every factual claim against sources, flags unsupported or fabricated-looking material, fixes non-neutral language and structure, and returns an edit report plus a corrected draft. Run it after a draft exists and before publication. It edits and verifies; it does not research new stories from scratch (delegate that to news-researcher). ([Boom-Vitt/claude-ai-news](https://github.com/Boom-Vitt/claude-ai-news/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-news/agents/news-editor.md))

Review-only: pins no edit tools, and keeps Bash only to run read-only git commands (diff / log / blame) for the workers -- never to mutate files.
